## My request:
What did Claude mess up? 


## assistant

Claude’s first version had several bugs that review caught:

- **Account switching:** one user’s saved study-tool state could appear after switching accounts.
- **Unsaved marks:** some highlights or annotations could be dropped.
- **Answer cross-outs:** crossing out a selected answer could leave the selection inconsistent.
- **RC persistence:** some Reading Comprehension tool changes weren’t reliably saved.
- **Touch controls:** cross-out controls could remain hidden on touch screens.

Claude revised the implementation twice, and I made additional fixes. The final combined version passed independent review, **311 tests, typecheck, and build**.

Those fixes are still unpublished. The last RC browser check remains unverified because the browser tool blocked access.

## assistant

The underlying issue was checking normal use without fully checking what happens when state changes: switching accounts, failing to save, clicking quickly, or using touch controls.

I saved explicit corrective instructions in [Claude’s memory](C:/Users/Joshu/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/lsatu-study-tool-transition-checks.md) and updated the delegation procedure. Future work orders must require:

- Account-switch isolation tests.
- Multiple edits while saving fails, with no lost work.
- Consistent selected-answer and cross-out behavior.
- Rapid-interaction and reload checks.
- Touch and keyboard verification.
- Evidence before claiming an existing feature is missing.

I verified the memory file and index were saved. An existing Claude session may need to reload them, so future work orders will include these requirements explicitly. Memory alone cannot guarantee prevention; tests and independent review remain required.

<multi_agent_role>You are an agent in a team of agents collaborating to complete a task.

You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents. All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.

When you provide a response in the final channel, that content is immediately delivered back to your parent agent.
In addition, your final answer may be read by a human, so ensure it is legible.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: NEW_TASK | MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
You may also see them addressed as to=/root/..., which indicates your identity is /root/...

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

} from \'react\';\\r\\nimport { useQuery } from \'@tanstack/react-query\';\\r\\nimport { useStudentSubjectScope } from \'@/hooks/useStudentSubjectScope\';\\r\\nimport { loadFirstAnswerLedger } from \'@/lib/firstAnswerSources\';\\r\\nimport { projectFirstAnswerProgress, type ProgressWindow } from \'@/lib/firstAnswerProgress\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/** One calculator for student Progress and Joshua\'s selected-student Insights. */\\r\\nexport function useFirstAnswerProgress(window: ProgressWindow, selectedStudentId?: string | null) {\\r\\n  const resolution = useStudentSubjectScope(selectedStudentId);\\r\\n  const scope = resolution.state === \'ready\' ? resolution.scope : null;\\r\\n  const query = useQuery({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\'first-answer-ledger\', scope?.actorId, scope?.studentId],\\r\\n    enabled: !!scope,\\r\\n    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,\\r\\n    queryFn: () => loadFirstAnswerLedger(scope!),\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  const progress = useMemo(() => query.data\\r\\n    ? projectFirstAnswerProgress({ ...query.data, window, asOf: new Date().toISOString() })\\r\\n    : null, [query.data, window]);\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    resolution,\\r\\n    progress: scope ? progress : null,\\r\\n    ledger: scope ? query.data ?? null : null,\\r\\n    accountId: scope ? query.data?.ownerId ?? null : null,\\r\\n    loading: resolution.state === React.createContext<UserSettingsContextType | undefined>(undefined);\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function UserSettingsProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {\\r\\n  const [settings, setSettings] = React.useState<UserSettings>(() => {\\r\\n    // Load from localStorage\\r\\n    const stored = localStorage.getItem(\'userSettings\');\\r\\n    if (stored) {\\r\\n      try {\\r\\n        return teachBackOnCorrect: false,\\r\\n      sectionDebriefEnabled: false,\\r\\n      storeFullTranscript: false,\\r\\n      defaultTimingMode: \'35\',\\r\\n      allowRepeats: false,\\r\\n      preferUnseen: true,\\r\\n      recycleAfterDays: 30\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  const updateSettings = React.useCallback((updates: Partial<UserSettings>) => {\\r\\n    setSettings(prev => {\\r\\n      const newSettings = { ...prev, ...updates };\\r\\n      localStorage.setItem(\'userSettings\', JSON.stringify(newSettings));\\r\\n      return newSettings;\\r\\n    });\\r\\n  }, []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <UserSettingsContext.Provider value={{ settings, updateSettings }}>\\r\\n      {children}\\r\\n    </UserSettingsContext.Provider>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function useUserSettings() {\\r\\n  const context = React.useContext(UserSettingsContext);\\r\\n  if (!context) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'useUserSettings must be used \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface WeeklyPlan {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  student_id: string;\\r\\n  created_by: string;\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  week_start: string;\\r\\n  week_end: string;\\r\\n  status: \'draft\' | \'published\' | \'archived\';\\r\\n  ai_generated: boolean;\\r\\n  rationale: string | null;\\r\\n  published_at: string | null;\\r\\n  created_at: string;\\r\\n  updated_at: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface WeeklyPlanItem {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  weekly_plan_id: string;\\r\\n  student_id: string;\\r\\n  classroom_item_id: string | null;\\r\\n  day_date: string;\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  instructions: string | null;\\r\\n  estimated_minutes: number | null;\\r\\n  unlock_at: string | null;\\r\\n  due_at: string | null;\\r\\n  status: \'assigned\' | \'in_progress\' | \'completed\' | \'skipped\';\\r\\n  sort_order: number;\\r\\n  metadata_json: Record<string, unknown>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Returns the current published weekly plan for the signed-in student,\\r\\n * plus its items grouped by date. RLS restricts students to published.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function useCurrentWeeklyPlan() {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  return useQuery<{ plan: WeeklyPlan | null; items: WeeklyPlanItem[] }>({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\'current-weekly-plan\', user?.id],\\r\\n    enabled: !!user,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => {\\r\\n      const { data: plans, error: pErr } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'weekly_plans\')\\r\\n        .select(\'*\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'status\', \'published\')\\r\\n        .order(\'week_start\', { ascending: false })\\r\\n        .limit(1);\\r\\n      if (pErr) throw pErr;\\r\\n      const plan = (plans?.[0] ?? null) as WeeklyPlan | null;\\r\\n      if (!plan) return { plan: null, items: [] };\\r\\n      const { data: items, error: iErr } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'weekly_plan_items\')\\r\\n        .select(\'*\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'weekly_plan_id\', plan.id)\\r\\n        .order(\'day_date\', { ascending: true })\\r\\n        .order(\'sort_order\', { ascending: true });\\r\\n      if (iErr) throw iErr;\\r\\n      return { plan, items: (items ?? []) as WeeklyPlanItem[] };\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Admin: full plan list for a student, including drafts.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function useStudentWeeklyPlans(studentId: string | null) {\\r\\n  return useQuery<WeeklyPlan[]>({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\'weekly-plans-admin\', studentId],\\r\\n    enabled: !!studentId,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => {\\r\\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'weekly_plans\')\\r\\n        .select(\'*\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'student_id\', studentId)\\r\\n        .order(\'week_start\', { ascending: false });\\r\\n      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n      return (data ?? []) as WeeklyPlan[];\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"9cce5f","wall_time_seconds":2.0259369,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1655,"output":"import { useMemo, useState } from \'react\';\\r\\nimport { Link } from \'react-router-dom\';\\r\\nimport { useFirstAnswerProgress } from \'@/hooks/useFirstAnswerProgress\';\\r\\nimport { firstAnswerDetails } from \'@/lib/firstAnswerDetails\';\\r\\nimport type { ProgressWindow } from \'@/lib/firstAnswerProgress\';\\r\\nimport { StudentIdentityUnavailableError } from \'@/lib/studentAccountScope\';\\r\\n\\r\\ntype AreaFilter = \'all\' | \'LR\' | \'RC\';\\r\\ntype ResultFilter = \'all\' | \'correct\' | \'missed\';\\r\\ntype AnswerFilter = \'all\' | \'first\' | \'repeat\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport default function FirstAnswerHistoryPanel({ window }: { window: ProgressWindow }) {\\r\\n  const { ledger, loading, error, resolution, refresh } = useFirstAnswerProgress(window);\\r\\n  const [area, setArea] = useState<AreaFilter>(\'all\');\\r\\n  const [result, setResult] = useState<ResultFilter>(\'all\');\\r\\n  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState<AnswerFilter>(\'all\');\\r\\n  const details = useMemo(() => ledger\\r\\n    ? firstAnswerDetails(ledger.answers, window, new Date().toISOString())\\r\\n    : null, [ledger, window]);\\r\\n  const filtered = useMemo(() => (details?.history ?? []).filter((row) =>\\r\\n    (area === \'all\' || row.area === area)\\r\\n    && (result === \'all\' || row.correct === (result === \'correct\'))\\r\\n    && (answer === \'all\' || row.isFirst === (answer === \'first\')),\\r\\n  ), [details, area, result, answer]);\\r\\n  const dateGroups = useMemo(() => {\\r\\n    const groups: { key: string; label: string; rows: typeof filtered }[] = [];\\r\\n    for (const row of filtered) {\\r\\n      const date = new Date(row.gradedAt);\\r\\n      const key = `${date.getFullYear()}-${date.getMonth()}-${date.getDate()}`;\\r\\n      const previous = groups[groups.length - 1];\\r\\n      if (previous?.key === key) {\\r\\n        previous.rows.push(row);\\r\\n      } else {\\r\\n        groups.push({\\r\\n          key,\\r\\n          label: date.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { year: \'numeric\', month: \'long\', day: \'numeric\' }),\\r\\n          rows: [row],\\r\\n        });\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return groups;\\r\\n  }, [filtered]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <section className=\\"mb-7 min-w-0 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-4 sm:p-5\\" aria-labelledby=\\"saved-answer-history-title\\">\\r\\n      <h2 id=\\"saved-answer-history-title\\" className=\\"text-lg font-semibold text-foreground\\">Saved answer history</h2>\\r\\n      <p className=\\"mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground\\">Completed, graded LR and RC answers in this window. Repeats appear here but do not enter first-answer accuracy.</p>\\r\\n      {loading ? <p className=\\"mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground\\" role=\\"status\\">Loading saved answers…</p>\\r\\n        : resolution.state === \'denied\' ? <p className=\\"mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">History is unavailable to this account.</p>\\r\\n        : error || !ledger || ledger.coverage.includedSources.length === 0 ? (\\r\\n          <div className=\\"mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground\\" role=\\"alert\\">\\r\\n            <p>{error instanceof StudentIdentityUnavailableError ? error.message : \'Saved answer history could not be loaded.\'}</p>\\r\\n            <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"mt-2 underline\\" onClick={() => void refresh()}>Retry</button>\\r\\n          </div>\\r\\n        ) : (\\r\\n          <>\\r\\n            {ledger.coverage.missingSources.length > 0 && (\\r\\n              <p className=\\"mt-3 text-xs text-amber-700\\" role=\\"status\\">Partial history: {ledger.coverage.missingSources.map((item) => item.source.replace(/_/g, \' \')).join(\', \')} unavailable.</p>\\r\\n            )}\\r\\n            <div className=\\"mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2\\">\\r\\n              <label className=\\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\\">Area <select aria-label=\\"History area\\" className=\\"ml-1 rounded border border-border bg-background px-2 py-1 text-foreground\\" value={area} onChange={(event) => setArea(event.target.value as AreaFilter)}><option value=\\"all\\">All</option><option value=\\"LR\\">LR</option><option value=\\"RC\\">RC</option></select></label>\\r\\n              <label className=\\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\\">Result <select aria-label=\\"History result\\" className=\\"ml-1 rounded border border-border bg-background px-2 py-1 text-foreground\\" value={result} onChange={(event) => setResult(event.target.value as ResultFilter)}><option value=\\"all\\">All</option><option value=\\"correct\\">Correct</option><option value=\\"missed\\">Missed</option></select></label>\\r\\n              <label className=\\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\\">Answer <select aria-label=\\"History answer\\" className=\\"ml-1 rounded border border-border bg-background px-2 py-1 text-foreground\\" value={answer} onChange={(event) => setAnswer(event.target.value as AnswerFilter)}><option value=\\"all\\">First and repeats</option><option value=\\"first\\">First only</option><option value=\\"repeat\\">Repeats only</option></select></label>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <p className=\\"mt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground\\">{filtered.length} saved {filtered.length === 1 ? \'answer\' : \'answers\'} match these filters.</p>\\r\\n            {filtered.length === 0 ? <p className=\\"mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">No saved answers match this window and these filters.</p> : (\\r\\n              <div className=\\"mt-2 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto rounded-lg border border-border\\">\\r\\n                {dateGroups.map((group) => (\\r\\n                  <section key={group.key} aria-label={group.label}>\\r\\n                    <h3 className=\\"sticky top-0 border-b border-border bg-muted px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-foreground\\">{group.label}</h3>\\r\\n                    <ol className=\\"divide-y divide-border\\">\\r\\n                      {group.rows.map((row) => (\\r\\n                        <li key={row.attemptId} className=\\"flex min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 p-3 text-xs\\">\\r\\n                          <div className=\\"min-w-0\\">\\r\\n                            <strong className=\\"block break-all text-foreground\\">{row.questionKey.replace(\':\', \' · \')}</strong>\\r\\n                            <span className=\\"text-muted-foreground\\">{row.topic ? `${row.topic} · ` : \'\'}{row.mode.replace(/-/g, \' \')}</span>\\r\\n                          </div>\\r\\n                          <span className=\\"text-muted-foreground\\">{row.correct ? \'Correct\' : \'Missed\'} · {row.isFirst ? \'First answer\' : \'Repeat\'}</span>\\r\\n                        </li>\\r\\n                      ))}\\r\\n                    </ol>\\r\\n                  </section>\\r\\n                ))}\\r\\n              </div>\\r\\n            )}\\r\\n            <div className=\\"mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-3 text-xs font-medium\\">\\r\\n              <Link className=\\"text-foreground underline\\" to=\\"/review/to-review\\">Open Review</Link>\\r\\n              <Link className=\\"text-foreground underline\\" to=\\"/practice/drills\\">Practice questions</Link>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n          </>\\r\\n        )}\\r\\n    </section>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 8.9 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"036612","wall_time_seconds":2.0383692,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1156,"output":"import { useMemo } from \'react\';\\r\\nimport { useNavigate } from \'react-router-dom\';\\r\\nimport { format, isSameDay, parseISO, isBefore, startOfDay } from \'date-fns\';\\r\\nimport { CheckCircle2, Circle, ArrowRight, Lock } from \'lucide-react\';\\r\\nimport { ClassroomSection, EmptyHint } from \'./ClassroomSection\';\\r\\nimport { useCurrentWeeklyPlan, type WeeklyPlanItem } from \'@/hooks/useWeeklyPlan\';\\r\\n\\r\\nconst DAYS_OF_WEEK = 7;\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction dayLabel(d: Date) {\\r\\n  if (isSameDay(d, new Date())) return \'Today\';\\r\\n  return format(d, \'EEE, MMM d\');\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function ThisWeekSection() {\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const { data, isLoading } = useCurrentWeeklyPlan();\\r\\n\\r\\n  const grouped = useMemo(() => {\\r\\n    if (!data?.plan) return null;\\r\\n    const start = parseISO(data.plan.week_start);\\r\\n    const days: { date: Date; items: WeeklyPlanItem[] }[] = [];\\r\\n    for (let i = 0; i < DAYS_OF_WEEK; i++) {\\r\\n      const date = new Date(start);\\r\\n      date.setDate(start.getDate() + i);\\r\\n      const items = data.items.filter((it) =>\\r\\n        isSameDay(parseISO(it.day_date), date),\\r\\n      );\\r\\n      days.push({ date, items });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return days;\\r\\n  }, [data]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <ClassroomSection\\r\\n      title=\\"This week\\"\\r\\n      hint={data?.plan ? data.plan.title : undefined}\\r\\n    >\\r\\n      {isLoading ? (\\r\\n        <EmptyHint>Loading your weekly plan…</EmptyHint>\\r\\n      ) : !data?.plan ? (\\r\\n        <EmptyHint>\\r\\n          No published plan yet. Check back after Joshua publishes one.\\r\\n        </EmptyHint>\\r\\n      ) : (\\r\\n        <div className=\\"space-y-2\\">\\r\\n          {grouped!.map(({ date, items }) => {\\r\\n            const isPast = isBefore(date, startOfDay(new Date())) && !isSameDay(date, new Date());\\r\\n            return (\\r\\n            <div\\r\\n              key={date.toISOString()}\\r\\n              className={`rounded-lg border border-border/60 bg-card/30 px-4 py-3 transition-opacity ${isPast ? \'opacity-40\' : \'\'}`}\\r\\n            >\\r\\n              <div className=\\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-2\\">\\r\\n                <div className=\\"text-xs font-semibold text-foreground/90\\">\\r\\n                  {dayLabel(date)}\\r\\n                </div>\\r\\n                {items.length === 0 && (\\r\\n                  <span className=\\"text-[11px] text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n                    Rest day\\r\\n                  </span>\\r\\n                )}\\r\\n              </div>\\r\\n              {items.length > 0 && (\\r\\n                <ul className=\\"space-y-1\\">\\r\\n                  {items.map((it) => {\\r\\n                    const locked = it.unlock_at && new Date(it.unlock_at) > new Date();\\r\\n                    const done = it.status === \'completed\';\\r\\n                    const Icon = done ? CheckCircle2 : locked ? Lock : Circle;\\r\\n                    return (\\r\\n                      <li key={it.id}>\\r\\n                        <button\\r\\n                          type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n                          disabled={!!locked}\\r\\n                          onClick={() =>\\r\\n                            it.classroom_item_id &&\\r\\n                            navigate(`/classroom/item/${encodeURIComponent(it.classroom_item_id)}`)\\r\\n                          }\\r\\n                          className=\\"w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-md text-left text-sm hover:bg-accent/40 disabled:opacity-60 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition ease-out active:scale-[0.99]\\"\\r\\n                        >\\r\\n                          <Icon\\r\\n                            className={\\r\\n                              done\\r\\n                                ? \'w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500 shrink-0\'\\r\\n                                : locked\\r\\n                                  ? \'w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground shrink-0\'\\r\\n                                  : \'w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground shrink-0\'\\r\\n                            }\\r\\n                          />\\r\\n                          <span className=\\"flex-1 truncate\\">{it.title}</span>\\r\\n                          {it.estimated_minutes ? return attempts.flatMap((attempt) => {\\r\\n    if (attempt.user_id !== studentId || !attempt.selected || typeof attempt.is_correct !== \'boolean\') return [];\\r\\n    const session = ## assistant

Recommended split is substantial and cleanly separated.

Claude #180 owns `UserSettingsContext`, `ProfileSection`, `ThisWeekSection`, `useWeeklyPlan`, and `TeacherWeeklyPlans`, plus dedicated helpers/tests/docs. Concrete issues found:

- Global, unvalidated `userSettings` can leak preferences between accounts.
- Current weekly plan query lacks explicit student filtering and current-week boundaries.
- Weekly-plan errors appear as “No published plan.”
- Missing assignment links leave inert enabled buttons.
- Status relies on icons; finish criteria are absent.
- Truncated titles and opacity40 past days impair scanning.
- Teacher new-day defaults invent a30-minute estimate.

Require account transitions, malformed/quota storage, cached failure/retry, actor scoping, week boundaries, reliable estimates, explicit status/finish, existing timing consequence tests. Unsupported feedback/analytics controls must remain disclosed rather than fabricated. Shared Home/MyWork hooks reserved.

Root #173 owns `firstAnswer*` libraries/hook/analytics panels, `ProgressTopics`, `DrillHistory`, `DossierProgress`, related tests/docs. Existing first-global-before-window/submission logic is already shipped. Biggest remaining work:

- Dense sortable, query-backed saved-answer History.
- Inspectable source/evidence IDs for numerator/denominator.
- Selected-student History and Topics sharing student calculations.
- Evidence-qualified topic priorities and supported contextual drill/review actions.
- Honest filter/taxonomy limitations.
- Partial-source uncertainty: a missing source can hide an earlier first answer, so available-only metrics must not imply complete first-ever accuracy.

All scopes and acceptance details sent to root. No edits made.

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"5d3fed","wall_time_seconds":0.0000231,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1428,"output":"import * as React from \'react\';\\r\\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  DEFAULT_SETTINGS,\\r\\n  claimLegacySettings,\\r\\n  normalizeSettings,\\r\\n  readSettingsFor,\\r\\n  writeSettingsFor,\\r\\n  type SettingsSource,\\r\\n  type UserSettings,\\r\\n} from \'@/lib/studySettings\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type { UserSettings } from \'@/lib/studySettings\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * saved      written to this browser for the signed-in account\\r\\n * unsaved    the browser refused the write; the value is in force in this tab\\r\\n *            only, and a retry writes the latest value\\r\\n * signed-out nobody to save for; defaults are in force\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport type SettingsSaveState = \'saved\' | \'unsaved\' | \'signed-out\';\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface UserSettingsContextType {\\r\\n  settings: UserSettings;\\r\\n  /** Applies at once. Returns whether the change was written to storage. */\\r\\n  updateSettings: (updates: Partial<UserSettings>) => boolean;\\r\\n  saveState: SettingsSaveState;\\r\\n  /** Writes the current value again. Returns whether it was written. */\\r\\n  retrySave: () => boolean;\\r\\n  /** The account these settings belong to, or null when signed out. */\\r\\n  ownerId: string | null;\\r\\n  /** False until auth has resolved and settings belong to the current account. */\\r\\n  ready: boolean;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface Snapshot {\\r\\n  ownerId: string | null;\\r\\n  settings: UserSettings;\\r\\n  saveState: SettingsSaveState;\\r\\n  source: SettingsSource;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction browserStorage(): Storage | null {\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    return typeof window === \'undefined\' ? null : window.localStorage;\\r\\n  } catch {\\r\\n    return null;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst UserSettingsContext = React.createContext<UserSettingsContextType | undefined>(undefined);\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function UserSettingsProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {\\r\\n  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const actorId = user?.id ?? null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Edits the browser refused to store, per account, for this tab\'s lifetime.\\r\\n  // Switching A -> B -> A brings A\'s unsaved edits back to A and never shows\\r\\n  // them to B. Signing out drops them all, since the device may be changing\\r\\n  // hands.\\r\\n  const unsavedRef = React.useRef(new Map<string, UserSettings>());\\r\\n\\r\\n  const load = React.useCallback((id: string | null): Snapshot => {\\r\\n    if (!id) return { ownerId: null, settings: { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS }, saveState: \'signed-out\', source: \'default\' };\\r\\n    const pending = unsavedRef.current.get(id);\\r\\n    if (pending) return { ownerId: id, settings: pending, saveState: \'unsaved\', source: \'saved\' };\\r\\n    const { settings, source } = readSettingsFor(browserStorage(), id);\\r\\n    return { ownerId: id, settings, saveState: \'saved\', source };\\r\\n  }, []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const [snapshot, setSnapshot] = React.useState<Snapshot>(() => load(actorId));\\r\\n\\r\\n  // An actual actor change swaps the whole snapshot in the same render, so no\\r\\n  // frame ever shows one account\'s settings while another is signed in.\\r\\n  let current = snapshot;\\r\\n  if (snapshot.ownerId !== actorId) {\\r\\n    current = load(actorId);\\r\\n    setSnapshot(current);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // The committed state every write builds on. Updated synchronously by\\r\\n  // `apply`, so two quick edits never persist a stale snapshot.\\r\\n  const latest = React.useRef(current);\\r\\n  latest.current = current;\\r\\n\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (actorId === null) unsavedRef.current.clear();\\r\\n  }, [actorId]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const apply = React.useCallback((owner: string | null, updates: Partial<UserSettings>): boolean => {\\r\\n    const base = latest.current;\\r\\n    // A callback created for a previous account must not write into this one.\\r\\n    if (base.ownerId !== owner) return false;\\r\\n    const settings = normalizeSettings({ ...base.settings, ...updates });\\r\\n    let next: Snapshot;\\r\\n    let written = false;\\r\\n    if (!owner) {\\r\\n      next = { ...base, settings, saveState: \'signed-out\' };\\r\\n    } else {\\r\\n      const storage = browserStorage();\\r\\n      written =\\r\\n        base.source === \'legacy\'\\r\\n          ? claimLegacySettings(storage, owner, settings)\\r\\n          : writeSettingsFor(storage, owner, settings);\\r\\n      if (written) unsavedRef.current.delete(owner);\\r\\n      else unsavedRef.current.set(owner, settings);\\r\\n      next = {\\r\\n        ownerId: owner,\\r\\n        settings,\\r\\n        saveState: written ? \'saved\' : \'unsaved\',\\r\\n        source: written ? \'saved\' : base.source,\\r\\n      };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    latest.current = next;\\r\\n    setSnapshot(next);\\r\\n    return written;\\r\\n  }, []);\\r\\n\\r\\n  // The device-wide settings from before accounts were separated belong to\\r\\n  // whoever signs in first. Claim them once so the next account does not.\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (current.ownerId && current.source === \'legacy\' && current.saveState === \'saved\') {\\r\\n      apply(current.ownerId, {});\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }, [apply, current.ownerId, current.source, current.saveState]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const ownerId = current.ownerId;\\r\\n  const updateSettings = React.useCallback(\\r\\n    (updates: Partial<UserSettings>) => apply(ownerId, updates),\\r\\n    [apply, ownerId],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  const retrySave = React.useCallback(() => apply(ownerId, {}), [apply, ownerId]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const value = React.useMemo<UserSettingsContextType>(\\r\\n    () => ({\\r\\n      settings: current.settings,\\r\\n      updateSettings,\\r\\n      saveState: current.saveState,\\r\\n      retrySave,\\r\\n      ownerId,\\r\\n      ready: authReady !== false && ownerId === actorId,\\r\\n    }),\\r\\n    [actorId, authReady, current.saveState, current.settings, ownerId, retrySave, updateSettings],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  return <UserSettingsContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</UserSettingsContext.Provider>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function useUserSettings() {\\r\\n  const context = React.useContext(UserSettingsContext);\\r\\n  if (!context) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'useUserSettings must be used within UserSettingsProvider\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return context;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"d8d22e","wall_time_seconds":0.0000207,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":2160,"output":"// Study settings: what each one is, where it is stored, and what it changes.\\r\\n//\\r\\n// Ownership and lifetime\\r\\n// - Owner: the signed-in account (auth user id). Settings never follow a\\r\\n//   device from one account to the next.\\r\\n// - Persistence: this browser\'s localStorage, one key per account. There is\\r\\n//   no server copy (no schema for one), so a second device starts from the\\r\\n//   defaults. The UI says so.\\r\\n// - Failure: a write that throws (private mode, full storage) leaves the\\r\\n//   in-memory value in force for this tab and marks it unsaved. Later edits\\r\\n//   build on that in-memory value, and a retry writes the latest value.\\r\\n// - Legacy: before accounts were separated, every account on a device shared\\r\\n//   one `userSettings` key. The first account to sign in on that device\\r\\n//   inherits it once (so a student\'s extra-time setting is not lost), and the\\r\\n//   key is marked claimed so a second account starts from the defaults.\\r\\n//\\r\\n// Consequences\\r\\n// - Timing is read when a section starts. A section already running keeps\\r\\n//   the clock it started with; past sections keep the time they were taken\\r\\n//   under.\\r\\n// - Pool settings are read when a drill builds its question list. They\\r\\n//   choose which questions come next and never edit past answers, scores or\\r\\n//   history.\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type TimingMode = \'35\' | \'52.5\' | \'70\' | \'unlimited\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface UserSettings {\\r\\n  // Stored for compatibility. No screen in the app reads these five today,\\r\\n  // so the Account page does not offer them as switches.\\r\\n  tutorEnabled: boolean;\\r\\n  voiceCoachEnabled: boolean;\\r\\n  showContrast: boolean;\\r\\n  teachBackOnCorrect: boolean;\\r\\n  sectionDebriefEnabled: boolean;\\r\\n  storeFullTranscript: boolean;\\r\\n  // Live settings.\\r\\n  defaultTimingMode: TimingMode;\\r\\n  allowRepeats: boolean;\\r\\n  preferUnseen: boolean;\\r\\n  recycleAfterDays: number;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: UserSettings = Object.freeze({\\r\\n  tutorEnabled: true,\\r\\n  voiceCoachEnabled: true,\\r\\n  showContrast: false,\\r\\n  teachBackOnCorrect: false,\\r\\n  sectionDebriefEnabled: false,\\r\\n  storeFullTranscript: false,\\r\\n  defaultTimingMode: \'35\',\\r\\n  allowRepeats: false,\\r\\n  preferUnseen: true,\\r\\n  recycleAfterDays: 30,\\r\\n}) as UserSettings;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const LEGACY_SETTINGS_KEY = \'userSettings\';\\r\\nexport const LEGACY_CLAIM_KEY = \'userSettings:legacyClaimedBy\';\\r\\nexport const RECYCLE_MIN_DAYS = 1;\\r\\nexport const RECYCLE_MAX_DAYS = 365;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function settingsKeyFor(userId: string): string {\\r\\n  return `userSettings:${userId}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst TIMING_MODES: readonly TimingMode[] = [\'35\', \'52.5\', \'70\', \'unlimited\'];\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function isTimingMode(value: unknown): value is TimingMode {\\r\\n  return typeof value === \'string\' && (TIMING_MODES as readonly string[]).includes(value);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** A recycle value is valid only as a whole number of days in range. */\\r\\nexport function parseRecycleDays(raw: unknown): number | null {\\r\\n  const n = typeof raw === \'number\' ? raw : typeof raw === \'string\' && raw.trim() !== \'\' ? Number(raw) : NaN;\\r\\n  if (!Number.isInteger(n) || n < RECYCLE_MIN_DAYS || n > RECYCLE_MAX_DAYS) return null;\\r\\n  return n;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Field-by-field repair of anything read from storage. A bad field falls back\\r\\n * to its default; good fields survive. Unknown keys are dropped.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function normalizeSettings(raw: unknown): UserSettings {\\r\\n  const src = raw && typeof raw === \'object\' && !Array.isArray(raw) ? (raw as Record<string, unknown>) : {};\\r\\n  const bool = (k: keyof UserSettings) =>\\r\\n    typeof src[k] === \'boolean\' ? (src[k] as boolean) : (DEFAULT_SETTINGS[k] as boolean);\\r\\n  // Numbers were once stored as strings by a form; accept \\"52.5\\" and 52.5.\\r\\n  const timing = typeof src.defaultTimingMode === \'number\' ? String(src.defaultTimingMode) : src.defaultTimingMode;\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    tutorEnabled: bool(\'tutorEnabled\'),\\r\\n    voiceCoachEnabled: bool(\'voiceCoachEnabled\'),\\r\\n    showContrast: bool(\'showContrast\'),\\r\\n    teachBackOnCorrect: bool(\'teachBackOnCorrect\'),\\r\\n    sectionDebriefEnabled: bool(\'sectionDebriefEnabled\'),\\r\\n    storeFullTranscript: bool(\'storeFullTranscript\'),\\r\\n    defaultTimingMode: isTimingMode(timing) ? timing : DEFAULT_SETTINGS.defaultTimingMode,\\r\\n    allowRepeats: bool(\'allowRepeats\'),\\r\\n    preferUnseen: bool(\'preferUnseen\'),\\r\\n    recycleAfterDays: parseRecycleDays(src.recycleAfterDays) ?? DEFAULT_SETTINGS.recycleAfterDays,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type SettingsSource = \'saved\' | \'legacy\' | \'default\' | \'unreadable\';\\r\\n\\r\\ntype StorageLike = Pick<Storage, \'getItem\' | \'setItem\'>;\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction safeGet(storage: StorageLike | null, key: string): string | null {\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    return storage ? storage.getItem(key) : null;\\r\\n  } catch {\\r\\n    return null;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction parse(json: string): unknown {\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    return JSON.parse(json);\\r\\n  } catch {\\r\\n    return undefined;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Read an account\'s settings. Never throws. `legacy` means the device-wide\\r\\n * key was used and should be claimed (see claimLegacySettings).\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function readSettingsFor(\\r\\n  storage: StorageLike | null,\\r\\n  userId: string | null,\\r\\n): { settings: UserSettings; source: SettingsSource } {\\r\\n  if (!userId) return { settings: { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS }, source: \'default\' };\\r\\n  const own = safeGet(storage, settingsKeyFor(userId));\\r\\n  if (own !== null) {\\r\\n    const value = parse(own);\\r\\n    return value === undefined\\r\\n      ? { settings: { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS }, source: \'unreadable\' }\\r\\n      : { settings: normalizeSettings(value), source: \'saved\' };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const claimedBy = safeGet(storage, LEGACY_CLAIM_KEY);\\r\\n  const legacy = safeGet(storage, LEGACY_SETTINGS_KEY);\\r\\n  if (legacy !== null && (claimedBy === null || claimedBy === userId)) {\\r\\n    const value = parse(legacy);\\r\\n    if (value !== undefined) return { settings: normalizeSettings(value), source: \'legacy\' };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return { settings: { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS }, source: \'default\' };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Write an account\'s settings. Returns false instead of throwing. */\\r\\nexport function writeSettingsFor(storage: StorageLike | null, userId: string, settings: UserSettings): boolean {\\r\\n  if (!storage) return false;\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    storage.setItem(settingsKeyFor(userId), JSON.stringify(settings));\\r\\n    return true;\\r\\n  } catch {\\r\\n    return false;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Move the device-wide settings into this account and mark them claimed. The\\r\\n * old key is left in place (nothing is deleted), but no other account will\\r\\n * read it once the claim is written.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function claimLegacySettings(storage: StorageLike | null, userId: string, settings: UserSettings): boolean {\\r\\n  if (!writeSettingsFor(storage, userId, settings)) return false;\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    storage!.setItem(LEGACY_CLAIM_KEY, userId);\\r\\n    return true;\\r\\n  } catch {\\r\\n    return false;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* ── consequences, in the words the Account page shows ─────────────────── */\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const TIMING_LABEL: Record<TimingMode, string> = {\\r\\n  \'35\': \'35:00, standard time\',\\r\\n  \'52.5\': \'52:30, time and a half\',\\r\\n  \'70\': \'70:00, double time\',\\r\\n  unlimited: \'No timer, a stopwatch counts up\',\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function timingConsequence(mode: TimingMode): string {\\r\\n  const clock =\\r\\n    mode === \'unlimited\'\\r\\n      ? \'Timed sections you start will run without a countdown.\'\\r\\n      : `Timed sections you start will give you ${mode === \'35\' ? \'35 minutes\' : mode === \'52.5\' ? \'52 minutes 30 seconds\' : \'70 minutes\'} as your standard section.`;\\r\\n  return `${clock} Practice-test paces are worked out from this too. A section already running keeps its clock, and past sections keep the time they were taken under.`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function poolConsequence(s: Pick<UserSettings, \'allowRepeats\' | \'preferUnseen\' | \'recycleAfterDays\'>): string {\\r\\n  const days = `${s.recycleAfterDays} day${s.recycleAfterDays === 1 ? \'\' : \'s\'}`;\\r\\n  if (s.allowRepeats && !s.preferUnseen) {\\r\\n    return \'Your next drill can draw from every question, including ones you have already answered.\';\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (s.allowRepeats && s.preferUnseen) {\\r\\n    return \'Your next drill uses questions you have never answered while any are left, then draws from every question.\';\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (s.preferUnseen) {\\r\\n    return `Your next drill uses questions you have never answered while any are left. After that, a question comes back ${days} after you last saw it.`;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return `Your next drill mixes questions you have never answered with ones you last saw more than ${days} ago.`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const POOL_HISTORY_NOTE =\\r\\n  \'These settings only choose which questions come next. Your past answers, scores and history do not change. Full sections and hand-picked question sets are always served whole.\';\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"55029f","wall_time_seconds":0.0000233,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1128,"output":"diff --git a/src/hooks/useWeeklyPlan.ts b/src/hooks/useWeeklyPlan.ts\\nindex 67c7c5e..33349e6 100644\\n--- a/src/hooks/useWeeklyPlan.ts\\n+++ b/src/hooks/useWeeklyPlan.ts\\n@@ -1,6 +1,8 @@\\n import { useQuery } from \'@tanstack/react-query\';\\n import { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\n import { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\\n+import { localDayISO } from \'@/lib/localDate\';\\n+import { pickPrescribedPlan, type PlanRelation } from \'@/lib/weeklyPlanWeek\';\\n \\n export interface WeeklyPlan {\\n   id: string;\\n@@ -34,36 +36,57 @@ export interface WeeklyPlanItem {\\n }\\n \\n /**\\n- * Returns the current published weekly plan for the signed-in student,\\n- * plus its items grouped by date. RLS restricts students to published.\\n+ * Returns the signed-in student\'s prescribed week: the published plan whose\\n+ * dates contain today, else the next upcoming one, else the latest past one.\\n+ * The student\'s own to-do plan (`__personal__`) is never picked, and the read\\n+ * is scoped to the student\'s own rows even for an account RLS lets see more.\\n  */\\n export function useCurrentWeeklyPlan() {\\n   const { user } = useAuth();\\n-  return useQuery<{ plan: WeeklyPlan | null; items: WeeklyPlanItem[] }>({\\n-    queryKey: [\'current-weekly-plan\', user?.id],\\n-    enabled: !!user,\\n+  const userId = user?.id;\\n+  return useQuery<{ plan: WeeklyPlan | null; items: WeeklyPlanItem[]; relation: PlanRelation | null }>({\\n+    queryKey: [\'current-weekly-plan\', userId],\\n+    enabled: !!userId,\\n     queryFn: async () => {\\n       const { data: plans, error: pErr } = await supabase\\n         .from(\'weekly_plans\')\\n         .select(\'*\')\\n+        .eq(\'student_id\', userId)\\n         .eq(\'status\', \'published\')\\n         .order(\'week_start\', { ascending: false })\\n-        .limit(1);\\n+        .limit(20);\\n       if (pErr) throw pErr;\\n-      const plan = (plans?.[0] ?? null) as WeeklyPlan | null;\\n-      if (!plan) return { plan: null, items: [] };\\n-      const { data: items, error: iErr } = await supabase\\n-        .from(\'weekly_plan_items\')\\n-        .select(\'*\')\\n-        .eq(\'weekly_plan_id\', plan.id)\\n-        .order(\'day_date\', { ascending: true })\\n-        .order(\'sort_order\', { ascending: true });\\n-      if (iErr) throw iErr;\\n-      return { plan, items: (items ?? []) as WeeklyPlanItem[] };\\n+      const picked = pickPrescribedPlan((plans ?? []) as WeeklyPlan[], localDayISO());\\n+      if (!picked) return { plan: null, items: [], relation: null };\\n+      const items = await fetchWeeklyPlanItems(picked.plan.id);\\n+      return { plan: picked.plan, items, relation: picked.relation };\\n     },\\n   });\\n }\\n \\n+async function fetchWeeklyPlanItems(planId: string): Promise<WeeklyPlanItem[]> {\\n+  const { data: items, error } = await supabase\\n+    .from(\'weekly_plan_items\')\\n+    .select(\'*\')\\n+    .eq(\'weekly_plan_id\', planId)\\n+    .order(\'day_date\', { ascending: true })\\n+    .order(\'sort_order\', { ascending: true });\\n+  if (error) throw error;\\n+  return (items ?? Opening a task goes to its page and changes nothing; status only\\r\\n * moves when the work itself is saved or the task is checked off.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * `audience=\\"teacher\\"` renders the same model on the Weekly Plans page, with\\r\\n * no student links, so both sides see the same week.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\ntype Audience = \'student\' | \'teacher\';\\r\\n\\r\\nconst RELATION_NOTE: Record<PlanRelation, string | null> = {\\r\\n  current: null,\\r\\n  upcoming: \'This plan starts soon. Nothing is published for this week yet.\',\\r\\n  past: \'This is your most recent plan. Nothing newer has been published yet.\',\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction StatusIcon({ task }: { task: PrescribedTask }) {\\r\\n  const cls = \'h-4 w-4 shrink-0\';\\r\\n  if (task.status === \'completed\') return <CheckCircle2 className={`${cls} text-emerald-600`} aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />;\\r\\n  if (task.status === \'skipped\') return <MinusCircle className={cls} aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />;\\r\\n  if (task.locked) return <Lock className={cls} aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />;\\r\\n  if (task.status === \'in_progress\') return <CircleDashed className={cls} aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />;\\r\\n  return <Circle className={cls} aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst timeFormat = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(\'en-US\', { weekday: \'short\', hour: \'numeric\', minute: \'2-digit\' });\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction TaskBody({ task }: { task: PrescribedTask }) {\\r\\n  const meta: string[] = [];\\r\\n  meta.push(task.estimateMinutes !== null ? `About ${formatMinutes(task.estimateMinutes)}` : \'No time estimate\');\\r\\n  if (task.locked && task.unlockAt) meta.push(`Opens ${timeFormat.format(new Date(task.unlockAt))}`);\\r\\n  else if (task.dueAt && task.status !== \'completed\' && task.status !== \'skipped\') {\\r\\n    meta.push(`Due ${timeFormat.format(new Date(task.dueAt))}`);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <>\\r\\n      <span className=\\"flex min-w-0 items-start gap-2\\">\\r\\n        <StatusIcon task={task} />\\r\\n        <span className=\\"grid min-w-0 gap-0.5\\">\\r\\n          <strong className=\\"font-medium\\">\\r\\n            <span className=\\"sr-only\\">Step </span>\\r\\n            {task.step}. {task.title}\\r\\n          </strong>\\r\\n          {task.instructions ? <small className=\\"whitespace-pre-line\\">{task.instructions}</small> : null}\\r\\n          <small>{meta.join(\' · \')}</small>\\r\\n        </span>\\r\\n      </span>\\r\\n      <span className=\\"student-work-status shrink-0\\">{task.statusLabel}</span>\\r\\n    </>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function PrescribedWeekView({\\r\\n  week,\\r\\n  relation = \'current\',\\r\\n  audience = \'student\',\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  week: PrescribedWeekModel;\\r\\n  relation?: PlanRelation;\\r\\n  audience?: Audience;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  const { pathname } = useLocation();\\r\\n  const { totals } = week;\\r\\n  const estimate = estimateSummary(totals);\\r\\n  const headingId = React.useId();\\r\\n  const teacher = audience === \'teacher\';\\r\\n  const note = teacher ? null : RELATION_NOTE[relation];\\r\\n\\r\\n  const renderTask = (task: PrescribedTask) => {\\r\\n    const linkable = !teacher && task.href && !task.locked;\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <li key={task.id} data-testid=\\"prescribed-task\\">\\r\\n        {linkable ? (\\r\\n          <Link\\r\\n            to={routeWithReturnTo(task.href as string, pathname)}\\r\\n            className=\\"student-work-row\\"\\r\\n            aria-label={`Open step ${task.step}, ${task.title}. ${task.statusLabel}.`}\\r\\n          >\\r\\n            <TaskBody task={task} />\\r\\n          </Link>\\r\\n        ) : (\\r\\n          <div className={teacher ? \'flex items-start justify-between gap-3 py-2\' : \'student-work-row\'}>\\r\\n            <TaskBody task={task} />\\r\\n          </div>\\r\\n        )}\\r\\n      </li>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <section\\r\\n      aria-labelledby={headingId}\\r\\n      className={teacher ? \'space-y-3 text-sm text-zinc-200\' : \'student-work-list prescribed-week\'}\\r\\n    >\\r\\n      <header className=\\"grid gap-1\\">\\r\\n        <h2 id={headingId} className=\\"text-base font-semibold\\">\\r\\n          {teacher ? week.title : `Your week: ${week.title}`}\\r\\n        </h2>\\r\\n        <p className=\\"student-destination-message\\">{week.rangeLabel}</p>\\r\\n        {note ? <p className=\\"student-destination-message\\">{note}</p> : null}\\r\\n        <p className=\\"student-destination-message\\" data-testid=\\"prescribed-week-counts\\">\\r\\n          {totals.completed} of {totals.required} done\\r\\n          {totals.inProgress > 0 ? `, ${totals.inProgress} in progress` : \'\'}\\r\\n          {totals.skipped > 0 ? `, ${totals.skipped} skipped` : \'\'}.\\r\\n          {estimate ? ` ${estimate}` : \'\'}\\r\\n        </p>\\r\\n        <p className=\\"student-destination-message\\" data-testid=\\"prescribed-week-finish\\">\\r\\n          {week.finished ? \'Finished. Every task this week shows Completed.\' : week.finishCriterion}\\r\\n        </p>\\r\\n        {teacher ? null : (\\r\\n          <p className=\\"student-destination-message\\">\\r\\n            Tasks are in the order Joshua set. (\\r\\n              <p className=\\"student-destination-message\\">No tasks.</p>\\r\\n            ) : (\\r\\n              <ul>{day.tasks.map(renderTask)}</ul>\\r\\n            )}\\r\\n          </li>\\r\\n        ))}\\r\\n        {week.outsideWeek.length > 0 ? (\\r\\n          <li>\\r\\n            <h3 className=\\"text-sm font-semibold\\">Moved outside this week</h3>\\r\\n            <ul>{week.outsideWeek.map(renderTask)}</ul>\\r\\n          </li>\\r\\n        ) : null}\\r\\n      </ol>\\r\\n    </section>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Student wrapper: reads the signed-in student\'s prescribed week. */\\r\\nexport default function PrescribedWeek() {\\r\\n  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useCurrentWeeklyPlan();\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"d041c7","wall_time_seconds":10.0157412,"session_id":38914,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}'}]

The builder keeps one\\r\\n// flat array of drafts; each day column shows the drafts for that day in\\r\\n// array order. These helpers keep that order meaningful and make it the order\\r\\n// that gets published.\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface DayDraft {\\r\\n  day: string; // yyyy-MM-dd\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Swap a draft with its neighbour on the same day. Drafts on other days keep\\r\\n * their positions, so moving Tuesday\'s second task never disturbs Monday.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function moveWithinDay<T extends DayDraft>(items: T[], index: number, direction: -1 | 1): T[] {\\r\\n  const target = items[index];\\r\\n  if (!target) return items;\\r\\n  const sameDay = items\\r\\n    .map((it, i) => ({ it, i }))\\r\\n    .filter(({ it }) => it.day === target.day)\\r\\n    .map(({ i }) => i);\\r\\n  const at = sameDay.indexOf(index);\\r\\n  const swapWith = sameDay[at + direction];\\r\\n  if (swapWith === undefined) return items;\\r\\n  const next = items.slice();\\r\\n  next[index] = items[swapWith];\\r\\n  next[swapWith] = target;\\r\\n  return next;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The sort_order to publish for each draft, by its array index: day first,\\r\\n * then the order shown inside that day. Students read plans by day then\\r\\n * sort_order, so this makes the published order the order Joshua saw.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function prescribedSortOrder<T extends DayDraft>(items: T[]): number[] {\\r\\n  const ranked = items\\r\\n    .map((it, i) => ({ day: it.day, i }))\\r\\n    .sort((a, b) => a.day.localeCompare(b.day) || a.i - b.i);\\r\\n  const order = new Array<number>(items.length);\\r\\n  ranked.forEach(({ i }, rank) => {\\r\\n    order[i] = rank;\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  return order;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Replacing a builder that already holds tasks needs an explicit yes. */\\r\\nexport function replaceNeedsConfirm(currentCount: number): boolean {\\r\\n  return currentCount > 0;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"04d0d0","wall_time_seconds":0.0000212,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":2725,"output":"// The prescribed week, as one model both sides read.\\r\\n//\\r\\n// A weekly plan is Joshua\'s prescription: which day each task belongs to and\\r\\n// the order he put them in. The student\'s scan (My Work) and the teacher\'s\\r\\n// review of a published plan (Weekly Plans) both build from this module, so\\r\\n// the two can never disagree about order, status, counts or time.\\r\\n//\\r\\n// Rules this module holds:\\r\\n// - Order is the teacher\'s: day first, then the sort_order he saved. Nothing\\r\\n//   here re-ranks tasks by urgency, difficulty or any automated suggestion.\\r\\n// - Status is the stored weekly_plan_items.status. Opening a task never\\r\\n//   changes it; this module only reads.\\r\\n// - A time estimate exists only when the teacher entered a positive number.\\r\\n//   Missing or zero estimates are counted as unknown and disclosed, never\\r\\n//   filled with a guess.\\r\\n// - Days are calendar dates (YYYY-MM-DD), read in the student\'s local\\r\\n//   timezone, the same way Home and the booking strip read them.\\r\\n\\r\\nimport type { WeeklyPlan, WeeklyPlanItem } from \'@/hooks/useWeeklyPlan\';\\r\\nimport { readTodoAction } from \'@/lib/todoActions\';\\r\\nimport { parseLocalDay } from \'@/lib/localDate\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The hidden plan that hosts a student\'s own to-do items. Not a prescription. */\\r\\nexport const PERSONAL_PLAN_TITLE = \'__personal__\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function isPersonalPlan(plan: Pick<WeeklyPlan, \'title\' | \'student_id\' | \'created_by\'>): boolean {\\r\\n  return plan.title === PERSONAL_PLAN_TITLE || plan.created_by === plan.student_id;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type PlanRelation = \'current\' | \'upcoming\' | \'past\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Pick the one prescribed week a student should scan: the published plan whose\\r\\n * dates contain today, else the soonest upcoming one, else the most recent\\r\\n * past one. The student\'s personal to-do plan is never a candidate.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function pickPrescribedPlan<P extends WeeklyPlan>(\\r\\n  plans: P[],\\r\\n  todayISO: string,\\r\\n): { plan: P; relation: PlanRelation } | null {\\r\\n  const candidates = plans.filter(\\r\\n    (p) => p.status === \'published\' && !isPersonalPlan(p) && isISODate(p.week_start),\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  if (candidates.length === 0) return null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const endOf = (p: P) => (isISODate(p.week_end) ? p.week_end : addDaysISO(p.week_start, 6));\\r\\n\\r\\n  const current = candidates\\r\\n    .filter((p) => p.week_start <= todayISO && todayISO <= endOf(p))\\r\\n    // Two published plans overlapping today: the later start is the newer\\r\\n    // prescription.\\r\\n    .sort((a, b) => b.week_start.localeCompare(a.week_start) || b.created_at.localeCompare(a.created_at))[0];\\r\\n  if (current) return { plan: current, relation: \'current\' };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const upcoming = candidates\\r\\n    .filter((p) => p.week_start > todayISO)\\r\\n    .sort((a, b) => a.week_start.localeCompare(b.week_start))[0];\\r\\n  if (upcoming) return { plan: upcoming, relation: \'upcoming\' };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const past = candidates\\r\\n    .filter((p) => endOf(p) < todayISO)\\r\\n    .sort((a, b) => b.week_start.localeCompare(a.week_start))[0];\\r\\n  return past ? { plan: past, relation: \'past\' } : null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type PlanTaskStatus = WeeklyPlanItem[\'status\'];\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface PrescribedTask {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  /** 1-based position in the teacher\'s order across the whole week. */\\r\\n  step: number;\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  instructions: string | null;\\r\\n  status: PlanTaskStatus;\\r\\n  statusLabel: string;\\r\\n  /** Teacher-entered minutes, or null when none was given. */\\r\\n  estimateMinutes: number | null;\\r\\n  dueAt: string | null;\\r\\n  /** True while unlock_at is still in the future. */\\r\\n  locked: boolean;\\r\\n  unlockAt: string | null;\\r\\n  /** Where tapping the task goes. Null when there is no page to open. */\\r\\n  href: string | null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface PrescribedDay {\\r\\n  iso: string;\\r\\n  label: string;\\r\\n  isToday: boolean;\\r\\n  isPast: boolean;\\r\\n  tasks: PrescribedTask[];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface PrescribedWeek {\\r\\n  planId: string;\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  weekStart: string;\\r\\n  weekEnd: string;\\r\\n  rangeLabel: string;\\r\\n  days: PrescribedDay[];\\r\\n  /** Tasks whose stored day falls outside the plan\'s seven days. */\\r\\n  outsideWeek: PrescribedTask[];\\r\\n  totals: {\\r\\n    tasks: number;\\r\\n    completed: number;\\r\\n    inProgress: number;\\r\\n    notStarted: number;\\r\\n    skipped: number;\\r\\n    /** Tasks that count toward finishing: everything not skipped. */\\r\\n    required: number;\\r\\n    /** Sum of known estimates for tasks that count. */\\r\\n    knownMinutes: number;\\r\\n    /** Required tasks with no estimate. */\\r\\n    unknownEstimates: number;\\r\\n    /** Known minutes left on required tasks that are not completed. */\\r\\n    knownMinutesLeft: number;\\r\\n  };\\r\\n  finished: boolean;\\r\\n  finishCriterion: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst STATUS_LABEL: Record<PlanTaskStatus, string> = {\\r\\n  assigned: \'Not started\',\\r\\n  in_progress: \'In progress\',\\r\\n  completed: \'Completed\',\\r\\n  skipped: \'Skipped\',\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function statusLabel(status: string): string {\\r\\n  return STATUS_LABEL[status as PlanTaskStatus] ?? \'Not started\';\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction normalizeStatus(status: string | null | undefined): PlanTaskStatus {\\r\\n  return status === \'in_progress\' || status === \'completed\' || status === \'skipped\' ? status : \'assigned\';\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function knownEstimate(minutes: number | null | undefined): number | null {\\r\\n  return typeof minutes === \'number\' && Number.isFinite(minutes) && minutes > 0 ? Math.round(minutes) : null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The page a plan task opens. A linked guided drill opens its start page and\\r\\n * linked homework opens its assignment page; neither starts the work or\\r\\n * changes a status. Anything else opens the task\'s own classroom page.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function planTaskHref(item: Pick<WeeklyPlanItem, \'metadata_json\' | \'classroom_item_id\'>): string | null {\\r\\n  const action = readTodoAction(item.metadata_json);\\r\\n  if (action.kind === \'drill\' && action.ref_id) return `/guided/${encodeURIComponent(action.ref_id)}`;\\r\\n  if (action.kind === \'homework\' && action.ref_id) return `/classroom/${encodeURIComponent(action.ref_id)}`;\\r\\n  if (action.kind === \'bootcamp\' && action.route?.startsWith(\'/bootcamp\')) return action.route;\\r\\n  if (item.classroom_item_id) return `/classroom/item/${encodeURIComponent(item.classroom_item_id)}`;\\r\\n  return null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function formatMinutes(total: number): string {\\r\\n  if (total < 60) return `${total} min`;\\r\\n  const h = Math.floor(total / 60);\\r\\n  const m = total % 60;\\r\\n  return m === 0 ? `${h} hr` : `${h} hr ${m} min`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst dayFormat = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(\'en-US\', { weekday: \'short\', month: \'short\', day: \'numeric\' });\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction dayLabelFor(iso: string): string {\\r\\n  const d = parseLocalDay(iso);\\r\\n  return d ? dayFormat.format(d) : iso;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction isISODate(value: string | null | undefined): value is string {\\r\\n  return typeof value === \'string\' && /^\\\\d{4}-\\\\d{2}-\\\\d{2}/.test(value);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction addDaysISO(iso: string, days: number): string {\\r\\n  const d = parseLocalDay(iso.slice(0, 10));\\r\\n  if (!d) return iso;\\r\\n  d.setDate(d.getDate() + days);\\r\\n  const y = d.getFullYear();\\r\\n  const m = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, \'0\');\\r\\n  const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, \'0\');\\r\\n  return `${y}-${m}-${day}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function buildPrescribedWeek(\\r\\n  plan: WeeklyPlan,\\r\\n  items: WeeklyPlanItem[],\\r\\n  now: Date,\\r\\n  todayISO: string,\\r\\n): PrescribedWeek {\\r\\n  const weekStart = plan.week_start.slice(0, 10);\\r\\n  const weekEnd = isISODate(plan.week_end) ? plan.week_end.slice(0, 10) : addDaysISO(weekStart, 6);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const ordered = items\\r\\n    .filter((it) => it.weekly_plan_id === plan.id)\\r\\n    .map((it, index) => ({ it, index }))\\r\\n    .sort(\\r\\n      (a, b) =>\\r\\n        a.it.day_date.slice(0, 10).localeCompare(b.it.day_date.slice(0, 10)) ||\\r\\n        (a.it.sort_order ?? 0) - (b.it.sort_order ?? 0) ||\\r\\n        a.index - b.index,\\r\\n    )\\r\\n    .map(({ it }) => it);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const tasks = ordered.map<PrescribedTask & { day: string }>((it, i) => {\\r\\n    const status = normalizeStatus(it.status);\\r\\n    return {\\r\\n      id: it.id,\\r\\n      step: i + 1,\\r\\n      day: it.day_date.slice(0, 10),\\r\\n      title: it.title?.trim() || \'Untitled task\',\\r\\n      instructions: it.instructions?.trim() || null,\\r\\n      status,\\r\\n      statusLabel: statusLabel(status),\\r\\n      estimateMinutes: knownEstimate(it.estimated_minutes),\\r\\n      dueAt: it.due_at,\\r\\n      unlockAt: it.unlock_at,\\r\\n      locked: !!it.unlock_at && new Date(it.unlock_at).getTime() > now.getTime(),\\r\\n      href: planTaskHref(it),\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  const span: string[] = [];\\r\\n  for (let iso = weekStart; iso <= weekEnd && span.length < 14; iso = addDaysISO(iso, 1)) span.push(iso);\\r\\n  const spanSet = new Set(span);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const strip = ({ day: _day, ...task }: PrescribedTask & { day: string }): PrescribedTask => task;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const days: PrescribedDay[] = span.map((iso) => ({\\r\\n    iso,\\r\\n    label: dayLabelFor(iso),\\r\\n    isToday: iso === todayISO,\\r\\n    isPast: iso < todayISO,\\r\\n    tasks: tasks.filter((t) => t.day === iso).map(strip),\\r\\n  }));\\r\\n  const outsideWeek = tasks.filter((t) => !spanSet.has(t.day)).map(strip);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const required = tasks.filter((t) => t.status !== \'skipped\');\\r\\n  const totals = {\\r\\n    tasks: tasks.length,\\r\\n    completed: tasks.filter((t) => t.status === \'completed\').length,\\r\\n    inProgress: tasks.filter((t) => t.status === \'in_progress\').length,\\r\\n    notStarted: tasks.filter((t) => t.status === \'assigned\').length,\\r\\n    skipped: tasks.filter((t) => t.status === \'skipped\').length,\\r\\n    required: required.length,\\r\\n    knownMinutes: required.reduce((sum, t) => sum + (t.estimateMinutes ?? 0), 0),\\r\\n    unknownEstimates: required.filter((t) => t.estimateMinutes === null).length,\\r\\n    knownMinutesLeft: required\\r\\n      .filter((t) => t.status !== \'completed\')\\r\\n      .reduce((sum, t) => sum + (t.estimateMinutes ?? 0), 0),\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const finished = totals.required > 0 && totals.completed === totals.required;\\r\\n  const finishCriterion =\\r\\n    totals.required === 0\\r\\n      ? \'There is nothing to finish in this plan.\'\\r\\n      : `This week is finished when all ${totals.required} task${totals.required === 1 ? \'\' : \'s\'} show Completed.` +\\r\\n        (totals.skipped > 0 ? \' Skipped tasks do not count.\' : \'\');\\r\\n\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    planId: plan.id,\\r\\n    title: plan.title?.trim() || \'Weekly plan\',\\r\\n    weekStart,\\r\\n    weekEnd,\\r\\n    rangeLabel: `${dayLabelFor(weekStart)} to ${dayLabelFor(weekEnd)}`,\\r\\n    days,\\r\\n    outsideWeek,\\r\\n    totals,\\r\\n    finished,\\r\\n    finishCriterion,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** One plain sentence on time, never claiming more than the teacher entered. */\\r\\nexport function estimateSummary(totals: PrescribedWeek[\'totals\']): string | null {\\r\\n  if (totals.required === 0) return null;\\r\\n  if (totals.knownMinutes === 0) return \'No time estimates were given for this week.\';\\r\\n  const base = `About ${formatMinutes(totals.knownMinutes)} in total, ${formatMinutes(totals.knownMinutesLeft)} left`;\\r\\n  if (totals.unknownEstimates === 0) return `${base}.`;\\r\\n  return `${base}, not counting ${totals.unknownEstimates} task${totals.unknownEstimates === 1 ?
