\'\' : \'s\'} from the curriculum.`,\\r\\n      );\\r\\n    } catch (e) {\\r\\n      toast.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : \'Could not read the curriculum\');\\r\\n    } finally {\\r\\n      setFilling(false);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const publish = async () => {\\r\\n    if (!studentId || !user) return;\\r\\n    if (items.length === 0) {\\r\\n      toast.error(\'Add at least one item\');\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if (items.some((it) => !it.title.trim())) {\\r\\n      toast.error(\'Every item needs a title\');\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    setCreating(true);\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const start = parseISO(weekStart);\\r\\n      const end = addDays(start, 6);\\r\\n\\r\\n      // 1) Create the weekly_plans row.\\r\\n      const { data: plan, error: pErr } = await (supabase as unknown as {\\r\\n        from: (t: string) => {\\r\\n          insert: (v: unknown) => {\\r\\n            select: () => {\\r\\n              single: () => Promise<{ data: { id: string } | null; error: { message: string } | null }>;\\r\\n            };\\r\\n          };\\r\\n        };\\r\\n      })\\r\\n        .from(\'weekly_plans\')\\r\\n        .insert({\\r\\n          student_id: studentId,\\r\\n          created_by: user.id,\\r\\n          title: title.trim() || \'Weekly plan\',\\r\\n          week_start: format(start, \'yyyy-MM-dd\'),\\r\\n          week_end: format(end, \'yyyy-MM-dd\'),\\r\\n          status: \'published\',\\r\\n          published_at: new Date().toISOString(),\\r\\n        })\\r\\n        .select()\\r\\n        .single();\\r\\n      if (pErr || !plan) throw pErr ?? new Error(\'Failed to create plan\');\\r\\n      const planId = plan.id;\\r\\n\\r\\n      // 1b) Curriculum-sourced items name a drill the student does not have\\r\\n      //     yet. Assign those now so each becomes a real guided_drill_assignment\\r\\n      //     -- which is what carries the level, and what the task link needs to\\r\\n      //     point at. notify:false because this plan sends its own single\\r\\n      //     summary below; seven bell entries for one week is the exact UX\\r\\n      //     step 4 already avoids.\\r\\n      const toAssign = items\\r\\n        .map((it, idx) => ({ it, idx }))\\r\\n        .filter(({ it }) => it.curriculumDrillId && !it.action);\\r\\n\\r\\n      const assignedActions = new Map<number, TodoAction>();\\r\\n      if (toAssign.length > 0) {\\r\\n        // Grouped by band so each group carries its own level override.\\r\\n        const byLevel = new Map<string, typeof toAssign>();\\r\\n        for (const row of toAssign) {\\r\\n          const key = row.it.curriculumLevel ?? \'__profile__\';\\r\\n          byLevel.set(key, [...(byLevel.get(key) ?? []), row]);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n\\r\\n        for (const [key, group] of byLevel) {\\r\\n          const result = await assignWorkBundle({\\r\\n            teacherId: user.id,\\r\\n            studentIds: [studentId],\\r\\n            items: group.map(({ it }) => ({\\r\\n              kind: \'guided_drill\' as const,\\r\\n              drillId: it.curriculumDrillId as string,\\r\\n            })),\\r\\n            levelOverrides:\\r\\n              key === \'__profile__\'\\r\\n                ? undefined\\r\\n                : { [studentId]: key as StudentLevel },\\r\\n            notify: false,\\r\\n          });\\r\\n\\r\\n          if (result.ok.length === 0) {\\r\\n            throw new Error(\\r\\n              result.failed[0]?.error ?? \'Could not assign the curriculum drills for this plan\',\\r\\n            );\\r\\n          }\\r\\n          const assignmentIds = result.ok[0].assignmentIds ?? [];\\r\\n          group.forEach(({ idx }, i) => {\\r\\n            const assignmentId = assignmentIds[i];\\r\\n            if (!assignmentId) return;\\r\\n            assignedActions.set(idx, {\\r\\n              kind: \'drill\',\\r\\n              ref_id: assignmentId,\\r\\n              route: `/guided/${assignmentId}`,\\r\\n            });\\r\\n          });\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      // 2) Insert weekly_plan_items first so each row has an id we can\\r\\n      //    reference from a classroom_items row.\\r\\n      // sort_order follows what the builder shows: day by day, and within a\\r\\n      // day the order Joshua arranged. The student\'s week reads the same order.\\r\\n      const sortOrder = prescribedSortOrder(items);\\r\\n      const itemRows = items.map((it, idx) => {\\r\\n        const dayDate = parseISO(it.day);\\r\\n        const unlockAt = startOfDay(dayDate).toISOString();\\r\\n        const dueAt = it.deadline ? new Date(it.deadline).toISOString() : endOfDay(dayDate).toISOString();\\r\\n        return {\\r\\n          weekly_plan_id: planId,\\r\\n          student_id: studentId,\\r\\n          day_date: it.day,\\r\\n          title: it.title.trim(),\\r\\n          estimated_minutes: it.estimatedMinutes ?? null,\\r\\n          sort_order: sortOrder[idx],\\r\\n          status: \'assigned\',\\r\\n          unlock_at: unlockAt,\\r\\n          due_at: dueAt,\\r\\n          metadata_json: (() => {\\r\\n            const action = it.action ?? assignedActions.get(idx);\\r\\n            return action ? { action } : {};\\r\\n          })(),\\r\\n        };\\r\\n      });\\r\\n\\r\\n      const { data: insertedItems, error: iErr } = await (supabase as unknown as {\\r\\n        from: (t: string) => {\\r\\n          insert: (v: unknown) => {\\r\\n            select: (s: string) => Promise<{ data: { id: string; day_date: string; title: string; unlock_at: string; due_at: string; estimated_minutes: number | null }[] | null; error: { message: string } | null }>;\\r\\n          };\\r\\n        };\\r\\n      })\\r\\n        .from(\'weekly_plan_items\')\\r\\n        .insert(itemRows)\\r\\n        .select(\'id,day_date,title,unlock_at,due_at,estimated_minutes\');\\r\\n      if (iErr) throw iErr;\\r\\n      if (!insertedItems) throw new Error(\'Failed to create plan items\');\\r\\n\\r\\n      // 3) For each weekly_plan_item, create a real classroom_items row\\r\\n      //    visible to the student. Back-link via classroom_item_id.\\r\\n      const failed: string[] = [];\\r\\n      for (const wpi of insertedItems) {\\r\\n        try {\\r\\n          const ci = await createClassroomItem({\\r\\n            studentId,\\r\\n            createdBy: user.id,\\r\\n            type: \'weekly_plan_task\',\\r\\n            title: wpi.title,\\r\\n            description:\\r\\n              (title.trim() ? `${title.trim()}` : \'Weekly plan\') +\\r\\n              (wpi.estimated_minutes ? ` · ~${wpi.estimated_minutes} min` : \'\'),\\r\\n            unlockAt: wpi.unlock_at,\\r\\n            dueAt: wpi.due_at,\\r\\n            sourceTable: \'weekly_plan_items\',\\r\\n            sourceId: wpi.id,\\r\\n            visibleToStudent: true,\\r\\n            status: \'assigned\',\\r\\n            metadata: {\\r\\n              weekly_plan_id: planId,\\r\\n              day_date: wpi.day_date,\\r\\n              estimated_minutes: wpi.estimated_minutes,\\r\\n            },\\r\\n          });\\r\\n          // Back-link weekly_plan_item -> classroom_items.id\\r\\n          await (supabase as unknown as {\\r\\n            from: (t: string) => {\\r\\n              update: (v: unknown) => {\\r\\n                eq: (k: string, v: string) => Promise<{ error: { message: string } | null }>;\\r\\n              };\\r\\n            };\\r\\n          })\\r\\n            .from(\'weekly_plan_items\')\\r\\n            .update({ classroom_item_id: ci.id })\\r\\n            .eq(\'id\', wpi.id);\\r\\n        } catch (e) {\\r\\n          console.error(\'[weekly-plan] classroom_items create failed\', { wpi, error: e });\\r\\n          failed.push(wpi.title);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (failed.length > 0) {\\r\\n        toast.error(\\r\\n          `Plan published but ${failed.length} item(s) did not become Classroom items: ${failed.join(\', \')}`,\\r\\n        );\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      // 4) One summary notification per published plan (not one per item —\\r\\n      //    flooding the bell with seven entries on a weekly plan is bad UX).\\r\\n      const notify = await notifyStudentOfClassroomItem({\\r\\n        adminUserId: user.id,\\r\\n        studentId,\\r\\n        title: `Weekly plan: ${title.trim() || \'Weekly plan\'}`,\\r\\n        description: `${items.length} item${items.length === 1 ? \'\' : \'s\'} this week. Open My Work to see the week.`,\\r\\n        route: \'/my-work/to-do\',\\r\\n        assignmentType: \'weekly_plan\',\\r\\n      });\\r\\n\\r\\n      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\'weekly-plans-admin\', studentId] });\\r\\n      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\'weekly-plan-items\'] });\\r\\n      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\'current-weekly-plan\'] });\\r\\n      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\'classroom-items\'] });\\r\\n      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\'classroom-items-admin\', studentId] });\\r\\n      setItems([]);\\r\\n\\r\\n      const summary = summarizeNotifyResult(notify);\\r\\n      if (notify.inbox_ok && notify.bell_ok && notify.email_ok) {\\r\\n        toast.success(summary);\\r\\n      } else {\\r\\n        toast.warning(summary, {\\r\\n          description: notify.email_error ?? notify.inbox_error ?? notify.bell_error,\\r\\n        });\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    } catch (e) {\\r\\n      const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : \'Failed to publish\';\\r\\n      toast.error(msg);\\r\\n    } finally {\\r\\n      setCreating(false);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"a6b4f9","wall_time_seconds":3.007936,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":2903,"output":"import * as React from \\"react\\";\\r\\nimport { Loader2, RefreshCw } from \\"lucide-react\\";\\r\\nimport { toast } from \\"sonner\\";\\r\\nimport { useAuth } from \\"@/contexts/AuthContext\\";\\r\\nimport { useUserSettings, type SettingsSaveState } from \\"@/contexts/UserSettingsContext\\";\\r\\nimport { QuestionPoolService } from \\"@/lib/questionPoolService\\";\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  POOL_HISTORY_NOTE,\\r\\n  RECYCLE_MAX_DAYS,\\r\\n  RECYCLE_MIN_DAYS,\\r\\n  TIMING_LABEL,\\r\\n  parseRecycleDays,\\r\\n  poolConsequence,\\r\\n  timingConsequence,\\r\\n  type TimingMode,\\r\\n} from \\"@/lib/studySettings\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Study settings on the Account page: section timing and the question pool.\\r\\n * Each control says what it changes and when, and the card says honestly\\r\\n * where the value is stored and whether the last change was stored.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Only settings something in the app actually reads are offered. The old AI\\r\\n * tutor and voice coach switches changed nothing anywhere, so they are gone\\r\\n * from this page; their stored values are kept untouched.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction Toggle({\\r\\n  checked,\\r\\n  onChange,\\r\\n  label,\\r\\n  describedBy,\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  checked: boolean;\\r\\n  onChange: (v: boolean) => void;\\r\\n  label: string;\\r\\n  describedBy?: string;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <button\\r\\n      type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n      role=\\"switch\\"\\r\\n      aria-checked={checked}\\r\\n      aria-label={label}\\r\\n      aria-describedby={describedBy}\\r\\n      className={`classroom-toggle${checked ? \\" classroom-toggle-on\\" : \\"\\"}`}\\r\\n      onClick={() => onChange(!checked)}\\r\\n    >\\r\\n      <i aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n    </button>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction PrefRow({\\r\\n  label,\\r\\n  description,\\r\\n  descriptionId,\\r\\n  children,\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  label: string;\\r\\n  description?: string;\\r\\n  descriptionId?: string;\\r\\n  children: React.ReactNode;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"classroom-account-pref-row\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"classroom-account-pref-copy\\">\\r\\n        <span>{label}</span>\\r\\n        {description ? <small id={descriptionId}>{description}</small> : null}\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n      {children}\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function SaveStatus({\\r\\n  state,\\r\\n  onRetry,\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  state: SettingsSaveState;\\r\\n  onRetry: () => void;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"classroom-account-card-note\\" role=\\"status\\" aria-live=\\"polite\\">\\r\\n      {state === \\"unsaved\\" ? (\\r\\n        <>\\r\\n          <span>\\r\\n            This browser would not store your last change. It still applies until you close this tab.\\r\\n          </span>{\\" \\"}\\r\\n          <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"classroom-account-ghost-button\\" onClick={onRetry}>\\r\\n            Try saving again\\r\\n          </button>\\r\\n        </>\\r\\n      ) : state === \\"signed-out\\" ? (\\r\\n        <span>Sign in to keep these settings.</span>\\r\\n      ) : (\\r\\n        <span>\\r\\n          Saved in this browser for your account. On another device or browser you start from the\\r\\n          defaults.\\r\\n        </span>\\r\\n      )}\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction RecycleDaysInput({\\r\\n  value,\\r\\n  onCommit,\\r\\n  describedBy,\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  value: number;\\r\\n  onCommit: (days: number) => void;\\r\\n  describedBy: string;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  const [draft, setDraft] = React.useState(String(value));\\r\\n  const [error, setError] = React.useState<string | null>(null);\\r\\n  const errorId = React.useId();\\r\\n\\r\\n  // A saved value that changes underneath (retry, another control) wins over\\r\\n  // an untouched draft.\\r\\n  React.useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    setDraft(String(value));\\r\\n    setError(null);\\r\\n  }, [value]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const commit = () => {\\r\\n    const days = parseRecycleDays(draft);\\r\\n    if (days === null) {\\r\\n      setError(`Enter a whole number from ${RECYCLE_MIN_DAYS} to ${RECYCLE_MAX_DAYS}. Still using ${value}.`);\\r\\n      return;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    setError(null);\\r\\n    if (days !== value) onCommit(days);\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"grid justify-items-end gap-1\\">\\r\\n      <input\\r\\n        type=\\"number\\"\\r\\n        inputMode=\\"numeric\\"\\r\\n        min={RECYCLE_MIN_DAYS}\\r\\n        max={RECYCLE_MAX_DAYS}\\r\\n        step={1}\\r\\n        className=\\"classroom-account-number\\"\\r\\n        value={draft}\\r\\n        onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}\\r\\n        onBlur={commit}\\r\\n        onKeyDown={(e) => {\\r\\n          if (e.key === \\"Enter\\") commit();\\r\\n          if (e.key === \\"Escape\\") {\\r\\n            setDraft(String(value));\\r\\n            setError(null);\\r\\n          }\\r\\n        }}\\r\\n        aria-label=\\"Days before a seen question can come back\\"\\r\\n        aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}\\r\\n        aria-describedby={error ? `${describedBy} ${errorId}` : describedBy}\\r\\n      />\\r\\n      {error ? (\\r\\n        <small id={errorId} role=\\"alert\\" className=\\"max-w-[16rem] text-right\\">\\r\\n          {error}\\r\\n        </small>\\r\\n      ) : null}\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction StudySettingsCards() {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const { settings, updateSettings, saveState, retrySave } = useUserSettings();\\r\\n  const [confirmReset, setConfirmReset] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n  const [resetting, setResetting] = React.useState(false);\\r\\n  const timingDescId = React.useId();\\r\\n  const poolDescId = React.useId();\\r\\n  const repeatsDescId = React.useId();\\r\\n  const unseenDescId = React.useId();\\r\\n  const recycleDescId = React.useId();\\r\\n\\r\\n  const retry = () => {\\r\\n    if (retrySave()) toast.success(\\"Saved\\");\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const resetPool = async () => {\\r\\n    if (!user) return;\\r\\n    setResetting(true);\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      await QuestionPoolService.resetPool(user.id);\\r\\n      toast.success(\\"Question pool reset\\");\\r\\n      setConfirmReset(false);\\r\\n    } catch {\\r\\n      // Leave the dialog open so the student can try again or back out.\\r\\n      toast.error(\\"Could not reset your question pool. Nothing was changed. Please try again.\\");\\r\\n    } finally {\\r\\n      setResetting(false);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <>\\r\\n      <section className=\\"classroom-account-card\\" aria-labelledby=\\"study-timing-heading\\">\\r\\n        <header className=\\"classroom-account-card-head\\">\\r\\n          <h2 id=\\"study-timing-heading\\">Section timing</h2>\\r\\n        </header>\\r\\n        <PrefRow\\r\\n          label=\\"Your standard section time\\"\\r\\n          description={timingConsequence(settings.defaultTimingMode)}\\r\\n          descriptionId={timingDescId}\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          <select\\r\\n            className=\\"classroom-account-select\\"\\r\\n            value={settings.defaultTimingMode}\\r\\n            onChange={(e) => updateSettings({ defaultTimingMode: e.target.value as TimingMode })}\\r\\n            aria-label=\\"Your standard section time\\"\\r\\n            aria-describedby={timingDescId}\\r\\n          >\\r\\n            {(Object.keys(TIMING_LABEL) as TimingMode[]).map((mode) => (\\r\\n              <option key={mode} value={mode}>\\r\\n                {TIMING_LABEL[mode]}\\r\\n              </option>\\r\\n            ))}\\r\\n          </select>\\r\\n        </PrefRow>\\r\\n        <SaveStatus state={saveState} onRetry={retry} />\\r\\n      </section>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <section className=\\"classroom-account-card\\" aria-labelledby=\\"study-pool-heading\\">\\r\\n        <header className=\\"classroom-account-card-head\\">\\r\\n          <h2 id=\\"study-pool-heading\\">Question pool</h2>\\r\\n        </header>\\r\\n        <p className=\\"classroom-account-card-note\\" id={poolDescId} aria-live=\\"polite\\">\\r\\n          {poolConsequence(settings)}\\r\\n        </p>\\r\\n        <PrefRow\\r\\n          label=\\"Allow repeats\\"\\r\\n          description=\\"Lets a drill use questions you have already answered.\\"\\r\\n          descriptionId={repeatsDescId}\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          <Toggle\\r\\n            checked={settings.allowRepeats}\\r\\n            onChange={(v) => updateSettings({ allowRepeats: v })}\\r\\n            label=\\"Allow repeats\\"\\r\\n            describedBy={`${repeatsDescId} ${poolDescId}`}\\r\\n          />\\r\\n        </PrefRow>\\r\\n        <PrefRow\\r\\n          label=\\"New questions first\\"\\r\\n          description=\\"Uses questions you have never answered before anything else, while any are left.\\"\\r\\n          descriptionId={unseenDescId}\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          <Toggle\\r\\n            checked={settings.preferUnseen}\\r\\n            onChange={(v) => updateSettings({ preferUnseen: v })}\\r\\n            label=\\"New questions first\\"\\r\\n            describedBy={`${unseenDescId} ${poolDescId}`}\\r\\n          />\\r\\n        </PrefRow>\\r\\n        <PrefRow\\r\\n          label=\\"Days before a seen question can come back\\"\\r\\n          description={\\r\\n            settings.allowRepeats\\r\\n              ? \\"Not used while repeats are allowed.\\"\\r\\n              : \\"Counted from the last time you saw the question.\\"\\r\\n          }\\r\\n          descriptionId={recycleDescId}\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          <RecycleDaysInput\\r\\n            value={settings.recycleAfterDays}\\r\\n            onCommit={(days) => updateSettings({ recycleAfterDays: days })}\\r\\n            describedBy={recycleDescId}\\r\\n          />\\r\\n        </PrefRow>\\r\\n        <p className=\\"classroom-account-card-note\\">{POOL_HISTORY_NOTE}</p>\\r\\n        <SaveStatus state={saveState} onRetry={retry} />\\r\\n        <div className=\\"classroom-account-card-foot\\">\\r\\n          <button\\r\\n            type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n            className=\\"classroom-account-ghost-button\\"\\r\\n            onClick={() => setConfirmReset(true)}\\r\\n          >\\r\\n            <RefreshCw size={12} aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n            Reset question pool\\r\\n          </button>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      </section>\\r\\n\\r\\n      {/* Reset-pool confirm, rendered in-tree so it stays on classroom tokens */}\\r\\n      {confirmReset ? (\\r\\n        <div\\r\\n          className=\\"classroom-account-dialog-veil\\"\\r\\n          role=\\"presentation\\"\\r\\n          onClick={() => {\\r\\n            if (!resetting) setConfirmReset(false);\\r\\n          }}\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          <div\\r\\n            className=\\"classroom-account-dialog\\"\\r\\n            role=\\"alertdialog\\"\\r\\n            aria-modal=\\"true\\"\\r\\n            aria-labelledby=\\"reset-pool-title\\"\\r\\n            aria-describedby=\\"reset-pool-body\\"\\r\\n            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}\\r\\n            onKeyDown={(e) => {\\r\\n              if (e.key === \\"Escape\\" && !resetting) setConfirmReset(false);\\r\\n            }}\\r\\n          >\\r\\n            <h3 id=\\"reset-pool-title\\">Reset your question pool?</h3>\\r\\n            <p id=\\"reset-pool-body\\">\\r\\n              Every question counts as new again for choosing what comes next. Your past answers,\\r\\n              scores and history stay exactly as they are. This can\'t be undone.\\r\\n            </p>\\r\\n            <div className=\\"classroom-account-dialog-actions\\">\\r\\n              <button\\r\\n                type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n                className=\\"classroom-account-ghost-button\\"\\r\\n                onClick={() => setConfirmReset(false)}\\r\\n                disabled={resetting}\\r\\n                autoFocus\\r\\n              >\\r\\n                Keep my pool\\r\\n              </button>\\r\\n              <button\\r\\n                type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n                className=\\"classroom-account-primary-button\\"\\r\\n                onClick={() => void resetPool()}\\r\\n                disabled={resetting}\\r\\n              >\\r\\n                {resetting ? (\\r\\n                  <Loader2 size={13} className=\\"classroom-spin\\" aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n                ) : null}\\r\\n                Reset pool\\r\\n              </button>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n          </div>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      ) : null}\\r\\n    </>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Keyed on the account that owns the settings, so drafts, open dialogs and\\r\\n * field errors from one account never survive into another.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport default function StudySettingsSection() {\\r\\n  const { ownerId, ready } = useUserSettings();\\r\\n  if (!ready) {\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <section className=\\"classroom-account-card\\">\\r\\n        <header className=\\"classroom-account-card-head\\">\\r\\n          <h2>Section timing</h2>\\r\\n        </header>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"classroom-skeleton-stack\\">\\r\\n          <span className=\\"classroom-skeleton\\" />\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      </section>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return <StudySettingsCards key={ownerId ?? \\"signed-out\\"} />;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

Read-only review found these concrete issues requiring a bounded fix pass:

1. **Legacy settings can leak across accounts.** Account-key save succeeds, claim-marker save fails, then another account imports the same anonymous legacy preferences. Marker read failures also appear as “unclaimed.” Preserve anonymous legacy untouched and default new account settings rather than auto-attributing them.

2. **Cached prescribed week disappears after failed refresh.** The wrapper returns error-only whenever `isError`, even with valid cached data. Keep the week visible and add a warning/retry.

3. **Twenty future plans can hide the current week.** Latest20 sorted descending is insufficient for current/nearest-upcoming selection. Use a complete scoped read or relation-specific queries.

4. **Teacher publication ignores backlink errors.** `weekly_plan_items.update(classroom_item_id)` returns an error that is discarded. The workflow can report successful publication with nonworking task links.

5. **Pending publication can clear another student’s draft.** Switching students while an older publish awaits can later execute `setItems([])` against the newly selected student. Guard local settlements by originating student and actor.

Coverage read: settings context/helper/cards, prescribed-week helper/view/query, teacher publish/builder, MyWork mounting, and new regression files. Current regressions omit the five transitions above. No teacher integration regression file existed at inspection.

`MyWorkPage` changes extend the initial reserved ownership; confirm the delegation allowlist includes them. No edits or builds performed. Findings and test requirements sent to root.

Here the order is:\\r\\n//\\r\\n//   1. plan      insert the plan as `draft` (students cannot see drafts)\\r\\n//   2. curriculum assign curriculum drills that are not yet assignments\\r\\n//   3. items     insert the plan\'s tasks (invisible while the plan is a draft)\\r\\n//   4. mirrors   create each task\'s Classroom row, hidden from the student\\r\\n//   5. links     point each task at its Classroom row, requiring a matched row\\r\\n//   6. reveal    make the Classroom rows visible, requiring every row matched\\r\\n//   7. publish   flip the plan to `published`, requiring the draft row matched\\r\\n//\\r\\n// Each stage records what it finished on the job. When a stage fails the job\\r\\n// stops there and keeps everything already written; nothing is deleted. A\\r\\n// retry resumes from the same job and skips what is done, so it never inserts\\r\\n// a second plan, task, Classroom row or assignment. The plan is acknowledged\\r\\n// as published only by the last stage, and only then may anyone notify the\\r\\n// student.\\r\\n//\\r\\n// Known limit: between `reveal` and `publish` the Classroom rows are visible\\r\\n// while the plan is still a draft. If `publish` fails there, the retry finishes\\r\\n// it; the teacher is told exactly that.\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { endOfDay, parseISO, startOfDay } from \'date-fns\';\\r\\nimport type { TodoAction } from \'@/lib/todoActions\';\\r\\nimport type { StudentLevel } from \'@/lib/studentLevel\';\\r\\nimport { prescribedSortOrder } from \'@/lib/weeklyPlanBuilder\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface PublishDraft {\\r\\n  day: string; // yyyy-MM-dd (local)\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  estimatedMinutes?: number;\\r\\n  deadline?: string;\\r\\n  action?: TodoAction;\\r\\n  curriculumDrillId?: string;\\r\\n  curriculumLevel?: StudentLevel | null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type PublishStage = \'plan\' | \'curriculum\' | \'items\' | \'mirrors\' | \'links\' | \'reveal\' | \'publish\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface PublishJob {\\r\\n  /** Who started it and for whom. Never changes after creation. */\\r\\n  teacherId: string;\\r\\n  studentId: string;\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  weekStart: string;\\r\\n  /** Snapshot of the builder at the moment Publish was pressed. */\\r\\n  drafts: PublishDraft[];\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Ids chosen before anything is written, so a retry after a write whose\\r\\n   * answer was lost finds the same rows instead of inserting new ones.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  planKey: string;\\r\\n  itemKeys: string[];\\r\\n  planId: string | null;\\r\\n  /** Curriculum assignments made, by draft index. */\\r\\n  actions: Record<number, TodoAction>;\\r\\n  /** weekly_plan_items ids, by draft index. */\\r\\n  itemIds: Record<number, string>;\\r\\n  /** classroom_items ids, by draft index. */\\r\\n  mirrorIds: Record<number, string>;\\r\\n  /** Draft indexes whose task row is acknowledged as pointing at its mirror. */\\r\\n  linked: Record<number, true>;\\r\\n  revealed: boolean;\\r\\n  published: boolean;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface PublishOutcome {\\r\\n  job: PublishJob;\\r\\n  ok: boolean;\\r\\n  failedAt?: PublishStage;\\r\\n  message?: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The writes, injected so the order and acknowledgments can be tested. Shared so every assign\\r\\n * surface reports the same outcome the same way.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function describeAssignResult(\\r\\n  result: AssignWorkResult,\\r\\n  title: string,\\r\\n): { level: \'success\' | \'warning\' | \'error\'; message: string } {\\r\\n  const { ok, failed } = result;\\r\\n  const students = (n: number) => `${n} student${n === 1 ? \'\' : \'s\'}`;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (ok.length === 0) {\\r\\n    return {\\r\\n      level: \'error\',\\r\\n      message: failed[0]?.error\\r\\n        ? `Could not assign \\"${title}\\": ${failed[0].error}`\\r\\n        : `Could not assign \\"${title}\\"`,\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (failed.length > 0) {\\r\\n    return {\\r\\n      level: \'warning\',\\r\\n      message: `Assigned \\"${title}\\" to ${students(ok.length)}. ${students(failed.length)} failed: ${failed[0].error ?? \'unknown error\'}`,\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const quiet = ok.filter((r) => r.notify && !(r.notify.inbox_ok && r.notify.bell_ok && r.notify.email_ok));\\r\\n  if (quiet.length > 0) {\\r\\n    return {\\r\\n      level: \'warning\',\\r\\n      message: `Assigned \\"${title}\\" to ${students(ok.length)}, but ${students(quiet.length)} were not fully notified.`,\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return { level: \'success\', message: `Assigned \\"${title}\\" to ${students(ok.length)}` };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Undo a half-written assign. Best effort: if the cleanup itself fails there is\\r\\n * nothing further to try, so it is logged loudly and swallowed rather than\\r\\n * masking the original error that sent us here.\\r\\n */\\r\\nasync function rollback(input: {\\r\\n  assignmentId: string | null;\\r\\n  classroomItemId: string | null;\\r\\n  table: string;\\r\\n  kind: AssignableItem[\'kind\'];\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  const { assignmentId, classroomItemId, table, kind } = input;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (classroomItemId) {\\r\\n    const { error } = await supabase.from(\'classroom_items\').delete().eq(\'id\', classroomItemId);\\r\\n    if (error) console.error(\'[assignWork] rollback left a classroom item\', { classroomItemId, error });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Bootcamps write no assignment row, so there is nothing else to undo.\\r\\n  if (!assignmentId || kind === \'bootcamp\') return;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(table as \'homework_assignments\' | \'guided_drill_assignments\' | \'activity_assignments\')\\r\\n    .delete()\\r\\n    .eq(\'id\', assignmentId);\\r\\n  if (error) console.error(\'[assignWork] rollback left an assignment row\', { table, assignmentId, error });\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

Stable round-one review confirms two material defects remain:

- **Publication actor isolation is incomplete.** Publisher keys and view epochs track only student identity. It neither checks the current teacher before each write nor suppresses origin notifications after actor changes. A job started by teacher A can continue under teacher B’s session, clear B’s builder, and notify with A’s attribution.

- **Curriculum assignment retry is unsafe.** `assignCurriculum` still calls `assignWorkBundle` without stable idempotency. A committed assignment with a lost response can duplicate on retry. That shared helper also creates visible Classroom rows before final plan acknowledgment and deletes partial records during rollback, contradicting this workflow’s draft visibility and preservation contract.

Safest bounded correction: require curriculum suggestions to be assigned through the existing assignment workflow first, then link verified saved assignments. Block publishing unassigned curriculum suggestions before any write; do not invoke the shared assignment helper. Add actor/epoch guards to publisher state, every next write, settlements, and notifications; keep stopped jobs scoped by teacher plus student.

The original settings legacy leakage, cached refresh disappearance, current-week crowd-out, scoped item reads, invalid date/status, and zero-rounded estimates appear corrected. No edits, tests, or builds performed.

## assistant

Final round-two review has two remaining material defects:

1. **A→B→A permits a stale notification.** `sameActor` checks only the current teacher ID. Returning to A before the old publish settles re-enables notification even though the actor changed twice. The new regression explicitly expects this behavior. Add a teacher-only epoch, capture it at start, and require unchanged epoch plus ID. Student-only changes can still notify under the same teacher.

2. **Edits made during publishing are lost.** Builder inputs, add/remove/reorder, and template loading remain enabled. A successful same-view publish executes `setItems([])`, discarding changes made after the published snapshot. Disable the builder during publishing, or clear only when its captured revision remains unchanged.

Curriculum publication now blocks unassigned suggestions before any write; the non-idempotent assignment helper was removed. Staged acknowledgment/retry, scoped reads, settings legacy fail-closed, and cached-week recovery look corrected.

No edits, tests, or builds performed.

## assistant

Final core review finds **no remaining material defect in the two takeover fixes**:

- Actor epoch increments only when the teacher changes. A→B→A now suppresses stale notification; student-only switching still permits notification under the unchanged teacher.
- Builder clearing uses the latest callback and compares current title, week, and items against the published snapshot. Late edits are retained.
- Unassigned curriculum suggestions stop before any write.
- Publication requires acknowledged stages, preserves partial records, and checks the current session before subsequent writes.

Coverage: reviewed every changed production file and new production helper in this slice, including Profile extraction, settings context/storage/cards, prescribed-week model/view/readers, MyWork mounting, teacher builder/publication, and publisher hook/dependencies. Reviewed regression coverage across those areas and the final actor-epoch/draft-acknowledgment additions. I did not execute tests/build or verify browser behavior.

Remaining disclosed limits:

- Stopped publication recovery exists only for the mounted page’s lifetime; reload loses the client recovery job while preserving saved drafts.
- Classroom rows become visible immediately before final plan publication. A failed final acknowledgment can leave visible tasks with a draft week.
- A request already sent before account switching cannot be recalled.
- Genuine teacher-to-student publication/retry and responsive browser acceptance remain unverified by this review.

Known generated churn is outside implementation review and must remain excluded from intended commits.
